[\[121b.2\]]pa rab rib dag gis ji snyed ji lta bar/_/yod pa don dam kun rdzob tshul gyis chos gang gzugs brnyan gzhan du snang gyur pa'i/_/sems can rnams la bsam pa bzhin du dri med thar pa'i thabs ni rnam pa gsum/_/nyon mongs bag chags bcas par bcom pa'i dri med yang dag byang chub mchog 
[\[121b.3\]]rtogs [#]pa/_/rnam rtog dra ba thams cad spangs pa'i dri med ye shes zer gyis thob gyur nas/_/gang gis lhun gyis grub par phyogs bcu rnams su rab tu ston kyang 'khor ba la/_/der mi rtog pas gnas pa med cing snying rje [#]mya ngan 'das la mi gnas gang /_/thabs dang shes rab chen pos yongs su 
[\[121b.4\]]gzung bas bdag gzhan don gyi mthar byon dang /_/gang yang dam pa'i chos gang bcom ldan de yis yang dag rtogs nas legs bstan pa/_/gang 'di legs par sgrub dang ldan pa de yis bdud rtsi'i [#]go 'phang zhi ba 'thob/_/gang zhig spong bar byed pa de ni sdug bsngal rgya mtshor yun ring shin tu 
[\[121b.5\]]'bying /_/slob dang mi slob 'bras bu la gnas lam de nas song dge 'dun gzhan gyi mchog_/gang zhig bde gshegs dge 'dun yon tan dri med rig pa'i brtul zhugs kyis bsgrub [#]pa/_/bla med bsod nams zhing la dge ba cung zad phra mo dag kyang 'dir byas na/_/sa dang nam mkha' rgya che ba ltar

[\[173a.3\]]pa'i mdo sde las brgya stong du mar bstan to/_/lhag pa'i sems las brtsams nas theg pa chen po'i chos las [#]dmigs pa zhes bya ba ni gang gi phyir byang chub sems dpa' rnams theg pa chen po'i chos la dmigs kyi/_nyan thos rnams ni ma yin no/_/theg pa chen po snang ba dang /_bsod nams thams cad 
[\[173a.4\]]yang dag par bsags pa dang /_ting nge 'dzin gyi rgyal po zhes bya ba la/_ting nge 'dzin smos pa ni 'di lta bu'i ting nge 'dzin sna tshogs las gang gi phyir nyan thos rnams la ni gcig tsam yang med do/_/gnyen pos rab tu dbye ba zhes bya ba ni gang gi phyir sgrib pa thams cad kyi gnyen por rnam par gnas pa ste/_

[\[173a.7\]]pa med pa ni mngon par shes pa bsam gtan gyis mngon par sgrub par byed pa'o/_/las kyis rab tu dbye ba zhes bya ba la/_g.yo bar byed pa ni 'jig rten gyi khams rnams g.yo bar byed pa'o 'bar bar byed pa ni de dag nyid 'bar bar byed pa'o/_/khyab par byed pa ni 'od kyis khyab par byed par blta

[\[173b.1\]]bar bya'o/_/ston pa ni gang gzhan gyis mi nus pa de'i 'od kyis [#]gzhan 'jig rten gyi khams du ma'i sangs rgyas la sogs pa mthong bar byed pa'o/_/gzhan gyi dngos por bsgyur ba ni sa la sogs pa rnams chu nyid la sogs par bsgyur bar blta bar bya'o/_/'gro ba ni skad cig gcig la 'jig rten

[\[174b.1\]]'das pa la gnas pa ma yin no zhes bya ba'i tha tshig go/_/gang mdo sde las dge slong dag nga ni srog gcod pa yin no zhes bya ba la sogs pa gsungs pa de dag gi dgongs pa gang gis gsungs pa de ston par byed pa ste/_ji ltar na 'dod pas log par spyod pa yin zhes bya ba la/_gang gi phyir

[\[190b.1\]
